PROPRIETARY SOFTWARE Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology - Leading Engineering Institution Kerala Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology (SCET) has achieved remarkable recognition as one of the top 15 engineering colleges in Kerala, moving up from rank 24 to rank 15 in 2025. This autonomous engineering college maintains NAAC B+ accreditation and NBA accreditation. Located in Kodakara, Thrissur, Kerala, with 1800+ students, 180+ faculty, 90%+ placement rate. Programs: Computer Science Engineering, Electronics Engineering, Biotechnology, Biomedical Engineering, AI & ML, Data Science, Cyber Security. Affiliated to APJ Abdul Kalam Technological University. Best engineering college Thrissur, Kerala autonomous KEAM GATE admission 2026.

chunk_34	Announcements Official announcements and notices Upskill, Teachers training program 3 days ago The CSE Department of Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology is organizing a three-day faculty development program titled “UPSKILL: AI Training for School Teachers 2026” on April 21, 22, and 23, from 9:30 AM to 4:00 PM. This hands-on training aims to equip school teachers with practical exposure to modern AI tools and their applications in education. Led by certified Intel AI experts, the program focuses on enhancing teaching methodologies through technology integration. The sessions will be conducted at the Multi media hall, and participants will receive certificates upon successful completion. Inauguration of the Upgraded High-Speed Campus Wi-Fi Network 3 days ago We are pleased to announce that the official inauguration of our college’s newly upgraded high-speed Wi-Fi network will be held on Monday, 20th April 2026 at 4:00 PM at the Administrative Block. This significant upgrade marks a major step towards enhancing digital connectivity across the campus, ensuring seamless internet access to support academic excellence, research, and innovation. The inauguration ceremony will be graced by our respected Manager, Msgr. Wilson Erathara, who will formally launch the upgraded network. Thinkable Orientation Session 4 weeks ago Step into Thinkable Orientation Session and discover how your ideas can evolve into assistive solutions that create real social impact, gain insights into the Assistive Innovation Hackathon, Thinkable – Where Ideas Enable Lives, conducted by Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology under STRIDE Initiative Session by: Mr. Robin Francis Junior Program Executive, K-DISC Workshop on Next generation Sequencing Data Analysis 1 month ago The Department
chunk_35	of Biotechnology at Sahrdaya College of Engineering & Technology is organizing a two-day workshop on Next Generation Sequencing (NGS) Data Analysis on 25th and 26th March 2026 at the MB Computer Centre. The session will be led by Mr. Ravisankar V, Senior Information Officer at Kerala Agricultural University, who brings extensive expertise in bioinformatics and data analysis. This workshop is designed to provide participants with hands-on experience and practical insights into NGS data processing, analysis pipelines, and interpretation of genomic data. Aimed at S6 Biotechnology students, the event will enhance their understanding of modern sequencing technologies and equip them with essential computational skills relevant to research and industry. Data Science Club Inaguration 1 month ago The Data Pulse – Data Science Club of Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology is being inaugurated to create a vibrant platform for students to explore the rapidly evolving field of data science and artificial intelligence. The club aims to foster innovation, analytical thinking, and practical learning through workshops, expert talks, competitions, and collaborative projects. The inauguration ceremony will feature an invited talk by Prof. Dr. Sabu M K, Professor, Department of Computer Applications, Cochin University of Science and Technology, on the topic “Retrieval Augmented Generation: Enhancing LLMs with External Data.” The session will provide valuable insights into emerging AI technologies and their real-world applications. Logo Design Competition 1 month ago ​🚀 Calling all Designers! 🚀 ​The Data Science Club is hosting a Digital Logo Design Competition! We invite students from Semesters 2, 4, and 6 to showcase their creativity. ​The Challenge: Design a logo reflecting inte

Voltx Workshop 2 months ago "The Department of Electrical and Electronics Engineering, in association with the project team SCET VAIDYUTH, is conducting a workshop titled VOLT X from 4th February to 27th February 2026 for S2 students. The workshop aims to provide basic-level technical training related to the development of a solar-powered autonomous electric vehicle. The training will include an introduction to the software tools used in the project, namely MATLAB and TinkerCAD, along with fundamental electrical concepts such as wiring practices, electrical components, types of motors, and their specifications. Students will also be introduced to the mechanical works and integrations involved in the project, giving them an overall understanding of how electrical, software, and mechanical systems are combined in a real-time application. Registration Fee : 30 Rs/- Registration link : https://forms.gle/fGjGobv49h4VWxET9 The faculty in charge for the workshop are Dr. V. Vijikala and Sreekanth S, Department of Electrical and Electronics Engineering." chunk_66	About Faculty & Staff Programmes Curriculum Facilities Achievements Outcomes Activities & Sports Association Newsletter Placements Internships HOD Message About Computer Science & Engineering The Department of Computer Science and Engineering at Sahrdaya College of Engineering and Technology is committed to excellence in education, research, and service. We prepare students to become leaders and innovators in the rapidly evolving field of computer science and engineering. Established 2009 NBA Accredited 400+ Students 20+ Faculty Vision To be a center of excellence in computer science and engineering education, fostering innovation, research, and ethical leadership to address global technological challenges and contribute to societal development. Mission To provide quality education in computer science and engineering through innovative teaching methodologies, cutting-edge research, industry collaboration, and comprehensive skill development to produce competent professionals and entrepreneurs. Department Overview The Computer Science and Engineering department offers comprehensive undergraduate and postgraduate programs designed to meet the evolving needs of the technology industry. Our curriculum combines theoretical foundations with practical applications, ensuring students are well-prepared for successful careers in software development, research, and entrepreneurship. With state-of-the-art laboratories, experienced faculty, and strong industry partnerships, we provide an environment that encourages innovation, critical thinking, and professional growth. Our graduates have consistently demonstrated excellence in various fields including artificial intelligence, data science, cybersecurity, and software engineering.
